Murph Prep 2026

2026-04-01 – 2026-05-25 | 8 weeks | 6 fully programmed training days/week

Completion Note

Every listed training day below is fully written with warm-up, timed strength/skill or workout structure, main conditioning, finisher/prehab when appropriate, intent, and scaling. No programmed day is hidden or collapsed.

CrossFit Blaze Class Rules

  • Every day includes a coach-led 8–10 minute warm-up.
  • Strength/skill blocks are on a class clock, not loose sets.
  • Main conditioning is programmed to last at least 8 minutes for strong athletes.
  • Finishers are controlled accessory/prehab/bodybuilding work, not second metcons.
  • Scaling preserves the intended stimulus.

Weekly Template

DayFocusIntent
Wednesday2:00 bike + squats, bridges, lunges, squat hold, barbell ramp.Progress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
Thursday2:00 row + band pull-aparts, scap push-ups, ring rows, DB press, hollow hold.Progress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
Friday3:00 machine + dead bugs, bird dogs, Cossack shifts, plank, practice round.Progress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
Saturday2:00 bike + good mornings, KB DLs, empty-bar RDLs, hang muscle reps, ramp.Progress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
Sunday2:00 machine + inchworms, med-ball DLs, step-back burpees, wall balls, practice rounds.Progress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
Monday3:00 partner machine + squats, ring rows, med-ball cleans, carry, split plan.Community, simple shared work

Week 1 — Base + tissue tolerance

Theme: Base + tissue tolerance. Full 6-day training week.

Timing mix: squat/upper/aerobic/hinge/mixed/partner with varied clocks.

Wednesday 4/1 — 2:00 bike + squats, bridges, lunges, squat hold, barbell ramp.

Intent: Base + tissue tolerance. Controlled effort.

🔥 Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 easy bike, 10 air squats, 10 glute bridges, 10 walking lunges, 20 sec squat hold, 2–3 empty-bar ramp sets.

💪 Strength / Skill

Every 3:00 for 15:00: 6 controlled front squats + 8 alternating step-ups, rest the remainder. Keep legs durable for Murph volume.

🚦 Conditioning

12:00 AMRAP: 12 wall balls, 12/10 cal bike, 10 box step-ups, 8 sit-ups.

🧱 Finisher

Every 90 sec for 9:00: 12 goblet cyclist squats + 20 sec wall sit, rest remainder.

📉 Scaling

  • Elite: Rx+ / advanced skill.
  • Standard: Rx class version.
  • Modified: -20–30% reps/load; simpler skill.
  • Foundation: technique first; light + simple.

✨ Inspirational Quote

“Show up sharp; the workout rewards the athlete who starts with intent.”

Thursday 4/2 — 2:00 row + band pull-aparts, scap push-ups, ring rows, DB press, hollow hold.

Intent: Base + tissue tolerance. Controlled effort.

🔥 Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 easy row, 10 band pull-aparts, 10 scap push-ups, 8 ring rows, 8 DB strict press, 20 sec hollow hold, 2 ramp sets.

💪 Strength / Skill

Every 3:00 for 15:00: 5 strict presses + 8 ring rows; finish each interval with 20 sec hollow or active hang, rest the remainder.

🚦 Conditioning

12:00 quality AMRAP: 5 strict/ring rows, 7 perfect push-ups, 10/8 cal ski, 12 DB floor press. No kipping fatigue.

🧱 Finisher

9:00 upper pump: 12 DB lateral raises + 12 band pressdowns + 12 curls, controlled reps.

📉 Scaling

  • Elite: Rx+ / advanced skill.
  • Standard: Rx class version.
  • Modified: -20–30% reps/load; simpler skill.
  • Foundation: technique first; light + simple.

✨ Inspirational Quote

“Small wins stack into the kind of strength nobody can fake.”

Friday 4/3 — 3:00 machine + dead bugs, bird dogs, Cossack shifts, plank, practice round.

Intent: Base + tissue tolerance. Controlled effort.

🔥 Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

3:00 easy machine, 10 dead bugs, 10 bird dogs, 10 Cossack shifts, 30 sec plank, 1 easy practice round.

💪 Strength / Skill

Aerobic skill block — Every 4:00 for 20:00: 3:00 zone-2 machine + 40 sec trunk/carry station (choose 40 sec plank/dead bugs or 50m suitcase carry), then rest/transition remainder.

🚦 Conditioning

30:00 sustainable: 400m easy run or 2:00 machine, 20 step-ups, 100m farmer carry, 15 med-ball cleans, 30 sec plank. Conversational pace.

🧱 Finisher

8:00 trunk quality: 30 sec plank + 12 dead bugs + 50m suitcase carry, rotate smoothly.

📉 Scaling

  • Elite: Rx+ / advanced skill.
  • Standard: Rx class version.
  • Modified: -20–30% reps/load; simpler skill.
  • Foundation: technique first; light + simple.

✨ Inspirational Quote

“Today is not about perfect; it is about honest effort repeated well.”

Saturday 4/4 — 2:00 bike + good mornings, KB DLs, empty-bar RDLs, hang muscle reps, ramp.

Intent: Base + tissue tolerance. Controlled effort.

🔥 Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 easy bike, 10 good mornings, 10 KB deadlifts, 10 empty-bar RDLs, 5 hang muscle cleans/snatches, 2 ramp sets.

💪 Strength / Skill

Every 3:00 for 15:00: RDL tempo for 3–6 reps, crisp positions, rest the remainder.

🚦 Conditioning

11:00 AMRAP: 14 Russian KB swings, 12/10 cal bike/ski, 10 reverse lunges/leg, 8 burpees step-back.

🧱 Finisher

Every 2:00 for 8:00: 12 barbell hip thrusts + 15 banded hamstring curls, rest remainder.

📉 Scaling

  • Elite: Rx+ / advanced skill.
  • Standard: Rx class version.
  • Modified: -20–30% reps/load; simpler skill.
  • Foundation: technique first; light + simple.

✨ Inspirational Quote

“The clock can measure the work, but your standards define it.”

Sunday 4/5 — 2:00 machine + inchworms, med-ball DLs, step-back burpees, wall balls, practice rounds.

Intent: Base + tissue tolerance. Controlled effort.

🔥 Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 machine, 8 inchworms, 10 med-ball deadlifts, 8 burpees step-back, 10 wall balls, 2 practice rounds of the workout movements.

💪 Strength / Skill

Skill primer — Every 90 sec for 9:00: 3–5 technical reps of the workout barbell/gymnastics movement, rest the remainder.

🚦 Conditioning

Murph-prep mixed piece — 3 rounds for time, 18:00 cap: 200m run or 1:00 machine, 8 pull-up/ring-row reps, 12 push-ups, 16 air squats. Target 12:00–16:00; never use a vest.

🧱 Finisher

8:00 bodybuilding balance: 12 DB curls + 12 DB floor press + 15 band pull-aparts, easy quality.

📉 Scaling

  • Elite: Rx+ / advanced skill.
  • Standard: Rx class version.
  • Modified: -20–30% reps/load; simpler skill.
  • Foundation: technique first; light + simple.

✨ Inspirational Quote

“A strong day begins when you decide your effort is non-negotiable.”

Monday 4/6 — 3:00 partner machine + squats, ring rows, med-ball cleans, carry, split plan.

Intent: Base + tissue tolerance. Controlled effort.

🔥 Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

3:00 partner machine rotation, 10 air squats, 10 ring rows, 10 med-ball cleans, 50m carry each, then 2:00 to review splitting strategy.

💪 Strength / Skill

Partner prep — 8:00 movement review: 2 easy practice rounds of 5 reps per workout movement; coaches assign teams and strategy before the clock starts.

🚦 Conditioning

Partner Murph-prep relay — 28:00 AMRAP, split evenly: 400m run or 2:00 machine, 30 box step-ups, 24 ring rows/pull-ups, 30 push-ups, 40 air squats. Smooth shared pacing; no vest.

🧱 Finisher

8:00 partner pump: alternate 10 DB curls + 10 triceps extensions + 50m carry.

📉 Scaling

  • Elite: Rx+ / advanced skill.
  • Standard: Rx class version.
  • Modified: -20–30% reps/load; simpler skill.
  • Foundation: technique first; light + simple.

✨ Inspirational Quote

“Do the simple things with pride and the hard things get simpler.”

2026-05-26 – 2026-07-19 | 8 weeks | 6 fully programmed training days/week

Completion Note

Every listed training day below is fully written with warm-up, timed strength/skill or workout structure, main conditioning, finisher/prehab when appropriate, intent, and scaling. No programmed day is hidden or collapsed.

CrossFit Blaze Class Rules

  • Every day includes a coach-led 8–10 minute warm-up.
  • Strength/skill blocks are on a class clock, not loose sets.
  • Main conditioning is programmed to last at least 8 minutes for strong athletes.
  • Finishers are controlled accessory/prehab/bodybuilding work, not second metcons.
  • Scaling preserves the intended stimulus.

Weekly Template

DayFocusIntent
Tuesday2:00 bike + squats, bridges, lunges, squat hold, barbell ramp.Progress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
Wednesday2:00 row + band pull-aparts, scap push-ups, ring rows, DB press, hollow hold.Progress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
Thursday3:00 machine + dead bugs, bird dogs, Cossack shifts, plank, practice round.Progress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
Friday2:00 bike + good mornings, KB DLs, empty-bar RDLs, hang muscle reps, ramp.Progress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
Saturday2:00 machine + inchworms, med-ball DLs, step-back burpees, wall balls, practice rounds.Progress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
Sunday3:00 partner machine + squats, ring rows, med-ball cleans, carry, split plan.Community, simple shared work

Week 1 — Recover & reset

Theme: Recover & reset. Full 6-day training week.

Timing mix: squat/upper/aerobic/hinge/mixed/partner with varied clocks.

Tuesday 5/26 — 2:00 bike + squats, bridges, lunges, squat hold, barbell ramp.

Intent: Recover & reset. Controlled effort.

🔥 Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 easy bike, 10 air squats, 10 glute bridges, 10 walking lunges, 20 sec squat hold, 2–3 empty-bar ramp sets.

💪 Strength / Skill

Every 3:00 for 15:00: 5–6 goblet squat tempo reps, build only if speed stays clean, rest the remainder.

🚦 Conditioning

For time, 12:00 cap: 4 rounds of 14 wall balls, 12/10 cal bike, 12 walking lunges. Target 8:30–11:00.

🧱 Finisher

Every 90 sec for 9:00: 12 goblet cyclist squats + 20 sec wall sit, rest remainder.

📉 Scaling

  • Elite: Rx+ / advanced skill.
  • Standard: Rx class version.
  • Modified: -20–30% reps/load; simpler skill.
  • Foundation: technique first; light + simple.

✨ Inspirational Quote

“Attack the next rep and make the last round your signature.”

Wednesday 5/27 — 2:00 row + band pull-aparts, scap push-ups, ring rows, DB press, hollow hold.

Intent: Recover & reset. Controlled effort.

🔥 Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 easy row, 10 band pull-aparts, 10 scap push-ups, 8 ring rows, 8 DB strict press, 20 sec hollow hold, 2 ramp sets.

💪 Strength / Skill

Every 3:00 for 15:00: 5 strict presses + 8 ring rows; finish each interval with 20 sec hollow or active hang, rest the remainder.

🚦 Conditioning

E2MOM x 6 / 12:00: 10 DB push press, 10 ring rows, 12/10 cal ski. Target 1:20–1:40.

🧱 Finisher

9:00 upper pump: 12 DB lateral raises + 12 band pressdowns + 12 curls, controlled reps.

📉 Scaling

  • Elite: Rx+ / advanced skill.
  • Standard: Rx class version.
  • Modified: -20–30% reps/load; simpler skill.
  • Foundation: technique first; light + simple.

✨ Inspirational Quote

“Attack the next rep because ordinary work done daily becomes uncommon.”

Thursday 5/28 — 3:00 machine + dead bugs, bird dogs, Cossack shifts, plank, practice round.

Intent: Recover & reset. Controlled effort.

🔥 Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

3:00 easy machine, 10 dead bugs, 10 bird dogs, 10 Cossack shifts, 30 sec plank, 1 easy practice round.

💪 Strength / Skill

Aerobic skill block — Every 4:00 for 20:00: 3:00 zone-2 machine + 40 sec trunk/carry station (choose 40 sec plank/dead bugs or 50m suitcase carry), then rest/transition remainder.

🚦 Conditioning

34:00 zone-2 grinder: 3:00 row/bike, 20 box step-ups, 100m suitcase carry, 12 light KB deadlifts, 30 sec side plank/side.

🧱 Finisher

8:00 trunk quality: 30 sec plank + 12 dead bugs + 50m suitcase carry, rotate smoothly.

📉 Scaling

  • Elite: Rx+ / advanced skill.
  • Standard: Rx class version.
  • Modified: -20–30% reps/load; simpler skill.
  • Foundation: technique first; light + simple.

✨ Inspirational Quote

“Attack your pace and strength will follow.”

Friday 5/29 — 2:00 bike + good mornings, KB DLs, empty-bar RDLs, hang muscle reps, ramp.

Intent: Recover & reset. Controlled effort.

🔥 Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 easy bike, 10 good mornings, 10 KB deadlifts, 10 empty-bar RDLs, 5 hang muscle cleans/snatches, 2 ramp sets.

💪 Strength / Skill

Every 3:00 for 15:00: RDL tempo for 3–6 reps, crisp positions, rest the remainder.

🚦 Conditioning

3 rounds for time, 13:00 cap: 18 KB swings, 18/15 cal bike/ski, 16 box step-ups, 12 deadlifts light/moderate. Target 9:00–12:00.

🧱 Finisher

Every 2:00 for 8:00: 12 barbell hip thrusts + 15 banded hamstring curls, rest remainder.

📉 Scaling

  • Elite: Rx+ / advanced skill.
  • Standard: Rx class version.
  • Modified: -20–30% reps/load; simpler skill.
  • Foundation: technique first; light + simple.

✨ Inspirational Quote

“Attack your pace because character shows up under fatigue.”

Saturday 5/30 — 2:00 machine + inchworms, med-ball DLs, step-back burpees, wall balls, practice rounds.

Intent: Recover & reset. Controlled effort.

🔥 Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 machine, 8 inchworms, 10 med-ball deadlifts, 8 burpees step-back, 10 wall balls, 2 practice rounds of the workout movements.

💪 Strength / Skill

Skill primer — Every 90 sec for 9:00: 3–5 technical reps of the workout barbell/gymnastics movement, rest the remainder.

🚦 Conditioning

13:00 AMRAP: 8 power cleans, 10 burpees, 12 wall balls, 14/11 cal row.

🧱 Finisher

8:00 bodybuilding balance: 12 DB curls + 12 DB floor press + 15 band pull-aparts, easy quality.

📉 Scaling

  • Elite: Rx+ / advanced skill.
  • Standard: Rx class version.
  • Modified: -20–30% reps/load; simpler skill.
  • Foundation: technique first; light + simple.

✨ Inspirational Quote

“Attack your pace until confidence becomes automatic.”

Sunday 5/31 — 3:00 partner machine + squats, ring rows, med-ball cleans, carry, split plan.

Intent: Recover & reset. Controlled effort.

🔥 Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

3:00 partner machine rotation, 10 air squats, 10 ring rows, 10 med-ball cleans, 50m carry each, then 2:00 to review splitting strategy.

💪 Strength / Skill

Partner prep — 8:00 movement review: 2 easy practice rounds of 5 reps per workout movement; coaches assign teams and strategy before the clock starts.

🚦 Conditioning

Partner chipper — For time, 30:00 cap: 100/80 cal row, 80 KB swings, 70 wall balls, 60 box step-ups, 50 sit-ups, 40 burpees. Split anyhow.

🧱 Finisher

8:00 partner pump: alternate 10 DB curls + 10 triceps extensions + 50m carry.

📉 Scaling

  • Elite: Rx+ / advanced skill.
  • Standard: Rx class version.
  • Modified: -20–30% reps/load; simpler skill.
  • Foundation: technique first; light + simple.

✨ Inspirational Quote

“Attack your pace even when nobody is counting for you.”

Week 5 — Intensify strength

Theme: Intensify strength. Full 6-day training week.

Timing mix: squat/upper/aerobic/hinge/mixed/partner with varied clocks.

Tuesday 6/23 — 2:00 bike + squats, bridges, lunges, squat hold, barbell ramp.

Intent: Intensify strength. Confident effort.

🔥 Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

Blaze Power & Athletic Capacity 2026

2026-07-20 – 2026-08-29 | 6 weeks | 6 fully programmed training days/week

Completion Note

Every listed training day below is fully written with warm-up, timed strength/skill or workout structure, main conditioning, finisher/prehab when appropriate, intent, and scaling. No programmed day is hidden or collapsed.

CrossFit Blaze Class Rules

  • Every day includes a coach-led 8–10 minute warm-up.
  • Strength/skill blocks are on a class clock, not loose sets.
  • Main conditioning is programmed to last at least 8 minutes for strong athletes.
  • Finishers are controlled accessory/prehab/bodybuilding work, not second metcons.
  • Scaling preserves the intended stimulus.

Weekly Template

DayFocusIntent
Monday2:00 bike + squats, bridges, lunges, squat hold, barbell ramp.Progress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
Tuesday2:00 row + band pull-aparts, scap push-ups, ring rows, DB press, hollow hold.Progress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
Wednesday3:00 machine + dead bugs, bird dogs, Cossack shifts, plank, practice round.Progress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
Thursday2:00 bike + good mornings, KB DLs, empty-bar RDLs, hang muscle reps, ramp.Progress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
Friday2:00 machine + inchworms, med-ball DLs, step-back burpees, wall balls, practice rounds.Progress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
Saturday3:00 partner machine + squats, ring rows, med-ball cleans, carry, split plan.Community, simple shared work

Week 1 — Landing mechanics + speed

Theme: Landing mechanics + speed. Full 6-day training week.

Timing mix: squat/upper/aerobic/hinge/mixed/partner with varied clocks.

Monday 7/20 — 2:00 bike + squats, bridges, lunges, squat hold, barbell ramp.

Intent: Landing mechanics + speed. Controlled effort.

🔥 Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

Standalone pagePDF

Blaze Engine & Barbell Mastery 2026

Aug 31, 2026 – Nov 29, 2026 | 13 Weeks | 6 programmed days/week

Member-Facing Explanation

“We rebuilt strength after Murph, then turned that strength into speed and power. This cycle turns that into real CrossFit capacity: better barbell cycling, stronger gymnastics positions, smarter pacing, and more confidence in classic workouts.”

Cycle Rules

  • Every day includes a coach-led 8–10 minute warm-up.
  • Strength and skill work stays on the class clock.
  • Conditioning density increases gradually, but no back-to-back redline days.
  • Gymnastics volume builds from positions and strict strength before high-rep fatigue.
  • Running stays optional/limited; machines, carries, sleds, and indoor shuttles are the default until the winter weather cycle.
  • Bodybuilding finishers support joints, muscle, and member buy-in.

Weekly Template

DayFocusIntent
MondaySquat + lower-body metconStrength maintenance and leg stamina
TuesdayUpper gymnastics + press/pullStrict strength, pulling capacity, shoulder durability
WednesdayAerobic base + trunkSustainable engine without redline
ThursdayHinge + barbell cyclingPosterior chain and clean/snatch cycling
FridayMixed CrossFit intensityClassic CrossFit feel, controlled chaos
SaturdayPartner/team capacityCommunity workout, shared work, simple movements

Phase: Phase 3 — Benchmark / Retest / Consolidate

Monday 11/23 — Squat Strength + Lower-Body Capacity

Intent: Squat Strength + Lower-Body Capacity.

🔥 Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 machine + 10 squats, 10 glute bridges, 10 lunges, 20 sec squat hold, 2 squat ramp sets.

💪 Strength / Skill

Every 3:00 for 18:00: 3 back squats or front squats, fast stand, rest the remainder.

🚦 Conditioning

10:00 AMRAP: 10 wall balls, 10/8 cal bike, 10 sit-ups. Keep this as a quality checkpoint/retest exposure, not a sloppy redline.

🧱 Finisher

Every 90 sec for 6:00: 12 goblet cyclist squats + 20 sec wall sit

📉 Scaling

  • Elite: Rx+ / advanced skill.
  • Standard: Rx class version.
  • Modified: -20–30% reps/load; simpler skill.
  • Foundation: technique first; light + simple.

✨ Inspirational Quote

“Attack your effort with the same pride you bring to PR day.”

Standalone pagePDF

Blaze Winter Engine 2026-2027

Nov 30, 2026 – Feb 28, 2027 | 13 Weeks | 6 programmed days/week

Member-Facing Explanation

“The weather is finally good enough to use the outside again. This cycle brings running back progressively while keeping strength, gymnastics, and CrossFit intensity in the program. The goal is a bigger engine without beating up knees, calves, or backs.”

Cycle Rules

  • Running returns gradually: short intervals first, then tempo pieces, then longer mixed workouts.
  • Every run has a machine option for scaling, weather, or injury.
  • Strength stays in the week, but the main adaptation is engine, pacing, and repeatability.
  • No sudden high-volume running spikes.
  • Bodybuilding/prehab finishers emphasize calves, tibialis, hamstrings, hips, trunk, and shoulders.
  • January/February can support Open-style fitness without making every Friday a test.

Weekly Template

DayFocusIntent
MondayLower strength + short engineMaintain squat/hinge strength
TuesdayUpper/gymnastics + intervalsPull/press durability with repeatable conditioning
WednesdayRun development / aerobic baseMain running progression day
ThursdayHinge/Oly + short metconPower and posterior chain
FridayOpen-style CrossFitMixed modal intensity with skill exposure
SaturdayPartner long engineOutdoor community piece when weather is good

Week 1 — Running Reintroduction + Aerobic Base

Week of Nov 30, 2026

Phase: Phase 1 — Running Reintroduction + Aerobic Base

Monday 11/30 — Squat Strength + Lower-Body Capacity

Intent: Squat Strength + Lower-Body Capacity.

🔥 Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 machine + 10 squats, 10 glute bridges, 10 lunges, 20 sec squat hold, 2 squat ramp sets.

💪 Strength / Skill

Every 3:00 for 18:00: 5 front squats or 5 back squats, leave 2 reps in reserve, rest the remainder.

🚦 Conditioning

E2MOM x 6: 150m run or 10/8 cal bike, 10 goblet squats, 8 sit-ups

🧱 Finisher

Every 90 sec for 6:00: 12 goblet cyclist squats + 20 sec wall sit + 15 tibialis raises to support running volume.

📉 Scaling

  • Elite: Rx+ / advanced skill.
  • Standard: Rx class version.
  • Modified: -20–30% reps/load; simpler skill.
  • Foundation: technique first; light + simple.

✨ Inspirational Quote

“Attack your effort because resilience is trained rep by rep.”

3:00 machine + 8 inchworms, 10 bird dogs/side, 10 step-ups, 30 sec couch stretch/side, 10 dead bugs/side. Add 2 x 100m relaxed run build-ups or machine build-ups.

💪 Strength / Skill

Run skill block — Every 3:00 for 18:00: 200m relaxed run or 12/10 cal bike, then walk/rest remainder. Keep nasal/conversational early; build only if mechanics stay clean.

🚦 Conditioning

Run intervals: 6 x 200m every 2:30; machine option 12/10 cal bike. Keep this as a quality checkpoint/retest exposure, not a sloppy redline.

🧱 Finisher

Every 1:00 for 6:00: odd minutes 40 sec suitcase hold, even minutes 15 tibialis raises + 10 dead bugs/side + 15 tibialis raises to support running volume.

📉 Scaling

  • Elite: Rx+ / advanced skill.
  • Standard: Rx class version.
  • Modified: -20–30% reps/load; simpler skill.
  • Foundation: technique first; light + simple.

✨ Inspirational Quote

“Honor your pace and leave no doubt about your effort.”

12 Days of Christmas 2026

2026-12-14 – 2026-12-25 | 2 weeks | 6 fully programmed training days/week

Completion Note

Every listed training day below is fully written with warm-up, timed strength/skill or workout structure, main conditioning, finisher/prehab when appropriate, intent, and scaling. No programmed day is hidden or collapsed.

CrossFit Blaze Class Rules

  • Every day includes a coach-led 8–10 minute warm-up.
  • Strength/skill blocks are on a class clock, not loose sets.
  • Main conditioning is programmed to last at least 8 minutes for strong athletes.
  • Finishers are controlled accessory/prehab/bodybuilding work, not second metcons.
  • Scaling preserves the intended stimulus.

Tuesday2:00 row + band pull-aparts, scap push-ups, ring rows, DB press, hollow hold.Progress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
Wednesday3:00 machine + dead bugs, bird dogs, Cossack shifts, plank, practice round.Progress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
Thursday2:00 bike + good mornings, KB DLs, empty-bar RDLs, hang muscle reps, ramp.Progress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
Friday2:00 machine + inchworms, med-ball DLs, step-back burpees, wall balls, practice rounds.Progress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue

🚦 Conditioning

Saturday 12/19 — Partner holiday chaos

🚦 Conditioning

🧱 Finisher

📉 Scaling

  • Elite: Rx+ / advanced skill.
